Checked 2026-10-01. 1771 companies in `COMPANY_LIST.md`. Full data: `status.csv`.

- **Apply now:** 293 open, not yet applied: 184 in your areas, 109 elsewhere (60 with a stated deadline)
- **Referrals / cold messages now:** 239 expected to open by 2026-11-26 or overdue vs last year
- **Tracker leads to confirm:** 0 listed by a GitHub tracker, company board not checked yet
- **Later / unknown timing:** 372
- **Already in TRACKER.md:** 21
- **No US new-grad SWE track found:** 765
- **Dropped (citizenship, green card, or clearance required):** 81 — kept in `status.csv` as `excluded`

"Apply now" only counts postings seen on the company's own job board. GitHub trackers (SimplifyJobs, speedyapply, vanshb03) are used for leads and last-cycle dates. Workday dates are the latest repost.
